Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any gravimetry module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 110. The projective resolution has length at most 737 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to gravimetry structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 110/737 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on gravimetry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 434. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 982 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by gravimetry generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 434, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 982. QED.
